                                                        Solea Model 3 Software Design Verification Protocol
                                                                                         790-00134 Rev. V




    Project Name:          Solea Model 3                         DHF Index #:            820-00003
    Author/Editor:         R. Scaer                              ECO/DCO#:               DCO-02212


PURPOSE:             The Solea Model 3 Software Design Verification Protocol dictates testing that will be
                     conducted to verify that the Solea software application (CUS-01188) has been designed
                     to satisfy set design input requirements and user needs.
SCOPE:               This protocol applies to the Solea Model 3 and is used as verification activity for system
                     configurations defined in TOP-00017 and TOP-00051. The latest revision of document
                     790-00155 describes the scope of the requirements which will be tested in the current
                     execution of this protocol. Unless otherwise specified, this protocol should be
                     executed on both TOP-00017 and TOP-00051 system configurations.

1      BACKGROUND
      1. The Solea system is a mobile, cart-based dental treatment system that uses pulsed laser energy.
         The Solea system utilizes advanced CO2 laser technology with a wavelength of 9.3 µm to safely
         and effectively perform:
             1. Ablation of hard tissue in the oral cavity for caries removal and cavity preparation.
             2. Incision, excision, vaporization, coagulation, and hemostasis of soft tissue in the oral
                 cavity.
             3. Cutting, shaving, contouring, and resection of oral osseous (bone) tissue.
             4. Aiding in the reduction of mineral loss in dental enamel.
      2. The terms “Solea”, “Solea Model 3”, “Solea M3”, “System” and “cart” may be used
         interchangeably through this protocol, respective report, and related documents.

7    METHODS
    1. Verification will be conducted via testing, using a Solea Model 3 cart.
    2. Testing should be conducted in temperature and humidity conditions typically found in an office
       computer environment as defined in Solea User Manual. Operating temperature should be 15 to
       30ºC and Relative Humidity should be 30% to 75% RH. Temperature and humidity logging is not
       required.
    3. While there is no plan for such methods in this protocol:
            1. Reference to other verification reports which satisfy the Software Requirement may be
                 used for verification, such as System Verification Report, or third-party testing. If
                 Software Requirements are satisfied through testing and passing of the system-level
                 requirements, a report will specify where and how each requirement was satisfied.
            2. Reference to released outputs, i.e. drawings, may be used, which will serve as verification
                 by inspection, analysis or rationale.
    4. If a test method instructs the user to cause a non-recoverable error: Disconnect the CAN-bus
       adapter USB port from the computer box.
    5. If a test method instructs the user to cause a recoverable error: Disconnect a DCMB fan.
    6. If a test method instructs the user to verify network connection of any kind (WiFi or Cellular),
       open the Windows command prompt and use the “ping 8.8.8.8 -t” command to make sure
       network communications are being enabled/disabled as expected.
    7. The system state referred to as “Arming State” is the combination of Mist Verification (when
       applicable) and Laser-Use-Warning states.

8    DOCUMENTATION
    1. All data collected must be noted on data sheets.
    2. All results of tests must be documented on the date that the test was conducted.
    3. Data sheets must be printed and stored in a binder during the testing execution process.
    4. Data sheets must be scanned and attached to the report upon completion of testing.
    5. Must list all equipment and materials used, brand/source/vendor, part number, serial number,
       calibration due dates, lot number.
            1. Serial Numbers and Calibration due date will be noted during testing and in report.
            2. Specific material used during testing must be noted in for each test method.
    6. If needed, an entry can be made in the Modification and/or Deviation Forms. In such case, on the
       original page of the data sheet, reference the page that the notes continue to. On the Modification
       and/or Deviation Form, reference which data sheet the notes are continued from.
    7. Any changes to the cart hardware, software, firmware, or electronics should be noted and
       reported in the report. Impact to previously testing to requirements will be evaluated for impact
       and retested as needed.
    8. Additional sections for test cases may be added throughout the protocol and must be shown in
       the report.

9    UNIT(S) UNDER TEST
    1. The unit(s) under test (UUT), including the versions for all the major components of the system
       required for testing, will be recorded in the UUT datasheet(s) provided in Section 13.3: Unit(s)
       Under Test (UUT). Before beginning testing, record serial no. of the cart under test, and versions
       of all the installed software and firmware applicable to testing.

10 REPORT RESULTS
   1. In the body of report, list every requirement ID, whether it passed, and a reference to where the
      result, analysis or reference can be found.
           1. If the result is quantitative, specifying that result is optional.
           2. If Software Requirements are satisfied through testing and passing of the system-level
               requirements, a report will specify where and how each requirement was satisfied.
   2. All notes, data sheets, references and analysis to report which are used to verify or attempt to
      verify a requirement. If using another report’s results as reference to verify a requirement, data
      sheets do not need to also be attached to this report for that requirement.
   3. Include a summary of protocol modifications or deviations, if any.

11 SAMPLE SIZE
All test methods will be executed on a single system unless otherwise specified in the method. A sample
size of N=1 is sufficient for this protocol because the software, given the same inputs and conditions
(settings and hardware) is deterministic and the result of any test or input will result in the same output
from the software.

12 ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA
The requirement description itself is considered the acceptance criteria for each requirement. If a
requirement requires reference to another protocol and/or report, the test results of those methods
must pass to be considered references in this protocol.

GENERAL REQUIREMENTS: INSTALLATION - UPGRADE PROCESS
REQUIREMENTS
       Req ID                                                    General Requirements: Installation - Upgrade Process
 SW-IN-3                The Solea software shall communicate with the Convergent Installation Server to validate the Solea device serial number before
                        allowing the software to be upgraded.
 SW-IN-4                Upon successful connection to the Convergent Installation Server and successful validation of the serial number, the Solea
                        application shall guide the user through the following steps related to installing a software update:
 SW-IN-4.1              If the serial number is valid, but the customer is not authorized to receive software updates, the user shall be informed they are not
                        eligible for software updates
 SW-IN-4.1.1            The user shall be informed how to contact Convergent to enable receiving software updates
 SW-IN-4.2              After validating the entered serial number is eligible to receive software updates, the Solea application shall connect to the
                        Convergent Installation Server to determine if a new software release is available
 SW-IN-4.3              If any software releases are available, the user shall be presented with information about the most recent available software release.
                        Note that the available release could be newer, older, or the same release as the software release currently installed on the Solea
                        device.
 SW-IN-4.3.1            If no releases are available on the software installation server, the user shall be prompted and shall acknowledge that their Software
                        Update session shall be terminated.
 SW-IN-4.4              After reviewing information about the available release, the user shall have the option to install the new software release or to
                        terminate their Software Update session.
 SW-IN-4.5              If the user selects to install the software update, they shall be provided a status screen displaying the progress of the software
                        installation:
 SW-IN-4.8              Upon successful download, validation of downloaded files, and successful installation of software, the software shall be restarted.
 SW-IN-4.6              The user shall have the option to cancel the installation at any time during the download of the software.
 SW-IN-4.6.1            The user shall be prompted to confirm they wish to cancel before the installation progress is cancelled
 SW-IN-4.7              The software shall track the successful completion of the steps of the installation progress and, if any step is not completed
                        successfully, the user shall be notified, the installation shall be aborted, and the user shall be prompted and acknowledge the
                        termination of the Software Update session.

                                                                                                                                                   Page 20 of 285
Verification Test Protocol Template, 731-00006 Rev. D

                                                        COMPANY PROPRIETARY AND CONFIDENTIAL
                                                                                                            Solea Model 3 Software Design Verification Protocol
                                                                                                                                             790-00134 Rev. V

 SW-IN-4.8.1              The downloaded software shall be validated prior to installation.

                          [DESIGN CONSTRAINT]
                          In the case of downloaded Windows installation (.MSI) files, the installation program for .MSI files automatically performs a file
                          validation when opening the file. No additional validation shall be required for .MSI files.
 SW-IN-4.19               If the installation process is interrupted by the user, by power failure, or any other unexpected event, the software update shall be
                          aborted and the previous version of Solea software shall remain installed
 SW-IN-2                  The software shall be upgradable by the user without the user having to interact with Customer Success.
 SW-IN-5                  The success, or failure status of installing a new software release shall be reported from the Solea device to the Convergent
                          Installation Server for future reference by Convergent customer support personnel.
 SW-IN-6                  After successful installation of software from the server, the versions of software shall be reported from the Solea device to the
                          Convergent software installation server for reference by Convergent customer support personnel.

       SW-IN-4.6, SW-IN-4.6.1, SW-IN-4.7, SW-IN-4.8.1, SW-IN-4.19, SW-IN-2, SW-IN-5, SW-IN-6

GENERAL REQUIREMENTS: SOLEA USER EXPERIENCE
REQUIREMENTS
       Req ID                                                         General Requirements: Solea User Experience
 SW-UE-1                The software shall limit user input to acceptable values.
 SW-UE-2                Where acceptable values are limited to discrete choices, the software shall allow the user to select from a list of possible choices.
 SW-UE-3                The software shall display a busy indication if the task takes longer than 5 seconds.
 SW-UE-4                If the software uses a pop-up dialog, the software shall not permit the user to access other software functions until the pop-up is
                        dismissed and the task is complete.
 SW-UE-5                If the user fails to enter data for a required field or if the user enters invalid data for a field that is not corrected through the other UE
                        usability requirements, the user shall be required to enter the missing information or correct the entered data before proceeding.

GENERAL REQUIREMENTS: SOLEA BROADCAST NOTIFICATION
REQUIREMENTS
       Req ID                                                     General Requirements: Solea Broadcast Notification
 SW-BC-1                The software shall connect and download new and update existing messages from the Convergent Message server after the system
                        has been idle in Standby for 15 minutes or longer and the user is logged out.
 SW-BC-1.1              The software shall be able to download and store a minimum of 100 messages.
 SW-BC-2                The user shall be notified if there are unread messages.
 SW-BC-2.1              The notification shall be displayed as soon as a new message has been downloaded.
 SW-BC-2.2              The number of unread messages shall be displayed to the user using the range 1-9.
                        Note: 9 is being displayed for 9 or more unread messages
 SW-BC-3                The software shall display a list of all available messages received.
 SW-BC-3.1              The message list shall be accessible from the Home screen with one click.
 SW-BC-3.2              The user shall be able to open any available message from the message list.
 SW-BC-3.3              The software shall distinguish between a read and unread message.
 SW-BC-6                A message shall be marked as read once it has been displayed.
 SW-BC-3.4              The message list shall display:
                        - The Sent Date
                        - Subject
                        - Text Header (The start of the message body)
 SW-BC-3.7              The software shall display the message when a message is selected in the message list.
 SW-BC-3.5              The message list shall be sorted by date by default to and show the newest message at the top.
 SW-BC-3.6              The software shall allow the user to sort the message list by:
                        - Sent Date
                        - Subject
 SW-BC-4                The display of a message shall contain the following items:
 SW-BC-4.1              Subject

                                                                                                                                             Page 46 of 285
Verification Test Protocol Template, 731-00006 Rev. D

                                                         COMPANY PROPRIETARY AND CONFIDENTIAL
                                                                                                         Solea Model 3 Software Design Verification Protocol
                                                                                                                                          790-00134 Rev. V

 SW-BC-4.2              Sent date
 SW-BC-4.3              Text body
 SW-BC-4.1.1            The subject shall allow for a minimum of 30 characters.
 SW-BC-4.3.1            The text body shall allow for a minimum of 1000 text-based characters
 SW-BC-4.3.2            The software shall be able to display a URL link as a QR code.
 SW-BC-5                A message has a date range associated with it and the software shall only allow display of message if the current date is within the
                        date range.
 SW-BC-5.1              A message shall be deleted if the current date exceeds the message date range.
 SW-BC-7                The software shall notify the Convergent Message server when a message was delivered to the Solea.
                        Note, this may occur next time the system goes online and not immediately.
 SW-BC-8                The software shall notify the Convergent Message server when a message was read on the Solea.
                        Note, this may occur next time the system goes online and not immediately.

GENERAL REQUIREMENTS: SYSTEM STARTUP/SHUTDOWN
REQUIREMENTS
       Req ID                                                      General Requirements: System Startup/Shutdown
 SW-SS-1                The user shall have the ability to shut down the software and the system, resulting in the software exiting and the PC and other
                        instrument hardware to be powered off.
 SW-SS-3                If the system loses its main power source and is running on battery backup power, the software shall shut down the system.
 SW-SS-3.1              The software shall notify the user that the system will shut down prior to the shutdown.
 SW-SS-4                The software shall support the ability to detect when a user pushes the system hardware Emergency Stop button.
 SW-SS-4.1              The software shall turn the laser simmer off when it detects that the Emergency Stop button has been pressed.
                        Note: Applies to LCD only.
 SW-SS-8                The software shall disable network communication on startup.
 SW-SS-9                The software shall stop TeamViewer on startup.
 SW-SS-10               The software shall initialize communication with the following devices on startup:
 SW-SS-10.1             Mist Module
 SW-SS-10.2             Airflow Module
 SW-SS-10.3             Coolant Module
 SW-SS-10.4             Laser Power Module
 SW-SS-10.5             DC Power Monitor Module
 SW-SS-11               The software shall display a login screen after startup requiring the user to enter a PIN.
 SW-SS-12               After startup the software shall update module firmware if the module firmware version differs from the version that is included in
                        the software.
 SW-SS-15               Cart updates are packaged and deployed as a single signed and encrypted bundle that includes a manifest.
 SW-SS-13               After startup the software shall verify cart integrity and authenticity.

                        Note: This verification is similar to a Power on Self Test (POST).

                                                                                                                                                Page 56 of 285
Verification Test Protocol Template, 731-00006 Rev. D

                                                         COMPANY PROPRIETARY AND CONFIDENTIAL
                                                                                                          Solea Model 3 Software Design Verification Protocol
                                                                                                                                           790-00134 Rev. V



 SW-SS-16               Cart executables and libraries shall not contain PDB (Program database debugging) files in release distributions.

SECURITY: USER MANAGEMENT
REQUIREMENTS
      Req ID                                                                    Security: User Management
 SW-UM-1                The software shall support the following roles:
 SW-UM-1.1              User.
 SW-UM-1.2              Service Technician.
 SW-UM-1.3              Administrator
 SW-UM-2                The software shall require the following attributes to be defined within the software:
 SW-UM-2.1              User PIN [length of 4 digits, numeric].
 SW-UM-3                The software shall have the user roles specified by [SW-UM-1] predefined.
 SW-UM-4                The software shall require a unique PIN for each predefined role.
 SW-UM-5                The software shall allow the unique PIN to be changed for each predefined role.
 SW-UM-6                There must always be at least one Administrator.
 SW-UM-7                The software shall provide a method to reset an Administrator PIN without having to login.

SECURITY: AUTHENTICATION AND SESSION MANAGEMENT
REQUIREMENTS
       Req ID                                                      Security: Authentication and Session Management
 SW-AU-1                The software shall prevent a User from interacting with any software other than the Solea Graphical User Interface.
 SW-AU-2                The software shall automatically log out the active user after a 15 minute period of inactivity.

                        Note: Inactivity defined in this context as no user interaction with the software via the GUI or the foot pedal.
 SW-AU-2.1              Unsaved settings shall not be saved when the software automatically log out.
 SW-AU-4                When calculating "activity", as referenced in [SW-AU-2], the software shall consider any user directed operation (selecting something
                        in the user interface, pressing the foot pedal, keeping the foot pedal at a constant rate) as "activity".
 SW-AU-5                The software shall provide the ability for a user to log out of the software.

 SW-AR-2                The software shall disable or hide from view the list of actions that the current active user is unauthorized to perform.
 SW-AR-3                The software shall support the ability of a User to perform the following list of actions:
 SW-AR-3.1              Log on.
 SW-AR-3.2              Perform laser cutting.

 SW-AR-3.4              View system software version information.
 SW-AR-3.5              View help information.
 SW-AR-3.6              Modify the system sound volume. (See SW-SO-4.)
 SW-AR-3.7              Restore "user default" settings for the currently logged in user.
 SW-AR-3.8              Change the User PIN (password).
 SW-AR-3.10             Log out of the software
 SW-AR-3.12             Perform and view results of system diagnostics tests (See SW-SDT-1.)
 SW-AR-3.13             Upload log files to the Convergent Log Server.
 SW-AR-3.14             Enable network communication.
 SW-AR-3.15             Adjust the Wi-Fi signal threshold used to determine when to switch over to 4G communication.
 SW-AR-4                The software shall support the ability of a user assigned to the Administrator role to perform the following list of action, in addition
                        to those assigned to the User role listed in SW-AR-3:
 SW-AR-4.1              Add and remove Users and other Administrators (not self)
 SW-AR-4.2              Download and install a software update from the Convergent Installation Server.

SECURITY: DATA SECURITY AND INTEGRITY
REQUIREMENTS
       Req ID                                                              Security: Data Security and Integrity
 SW-DS-1                The software shall store credentials to third party systems, such as API keys, and API tokens in an encrypted format (not clear-text).
 SW-DS-2                The database containing configuration settings shall be encrypted.
 SW-DS-3                The database containing configuration settings shall be checked on startup and if it is corrupt the software shall:
 SW-DS-3.1              Save the corrupt database under a new name.
 SW-DS-3.2              Attempt to restore the database using the backup.
 SW-DS-3.3              If restoring the backup fail the software shall:
 SW-DS-3.3.1            Create a new database.
 SW-DS-3.3.2            Notify the user that a database was corrupt and had to be recreated.
 SW-DS-4                The software shall backup the database containing user settings on startup.
 SW-DS-2.1              The database containing laser pattern files shall be immutable and signed.

SW-DS-1, SW-DS-2, SW-DS-3.1, SW-DS-3.2, SW-DS-3.3, SW-DS-3.3.1, SW-DS-3.3.2, SW-DS-4, SW-DS-2.1

SECURITY: MALWARE HANDLING
REQUIREMENTS
      Req ID                                                                  Security: Malware Handling
 SW-MHR-1               The software shall periodically retrieve definition updates for malware scanning
 SW-MHR-2               The software shall periodically query and report the status of recent virus or malware scans
 SW-MHR-2.1             The report shall:
 SW-MHR-2.1.1           Be bundled with regularly uploaded system log files
 SW-MHR-2.1.2           Contain all threat detection events since the report was last run.
 SW-MHR-2.2             When the query detects an active threat, it shall:
 SW-MHR-2.2.1           Immediately attempt to remediate the threat via the installed system’s Malware Handling tool
 SW-MHR-3               If the software detect a threat that is not remediated the software shall:
 SW-MHR-3.1             Log the threat.
 SW-MHR-3.2             Log the current WiFi fallback setting.
 SW-MHR-3.3             Disable WiFi and force 4G connectivity.
 SW-MHR-3.4             Notify user.
 SW-MHR-3.5             Prevent use of the laser.

SW-MHR-1, SW-MHR-2, SW-MHR-2.1, SW-MHR-2.1.1, SW-MHR-2.1.2, SW-MHR-2.2, SW-MHR-2.2.1, SW-
     MHR-3, SW-MHR-3.1, SW-MHR-3.2, SW-MHR-3.3, SW-MHR-3.4, SW-MHR-3.5

NETWORKING CONNECTIVITY - WIFI AND 4G
REQUIREMENTS
       Req ID                                                           Networking Connectivity - WiFi and 4G
 SW-NC-1                The software shall communicate to external software components through Wi-Fi.
 SW-NC-2                The software shall communicate to external software components through cellular communication
 SW-NC-3                The software shall prioritize communication through Wi-Fi over cellular communication.
 SW-NC-4                The software shall allow the user to re-configure Wi-Fi network by allowing the user to make the following selections:
 SW-NC-4.1              Allow the user to select the default Wi-Fi network from a list of available networks.
 SW-NC-4.2              Allow the user to enter the network security key for the selected Wi-Fi network.
 SW-NC-5                The software shall provide networking tests:
 SW-NC-5.1              Internet connectivity using WiFi
 SW-NC-5.2              Internet connectivity using cellular modem
 SW-NC-5.3              Convergent Dental cloud gateway connectivity test
 SW-NC-5.4              Amazon S3 connectivity
 SW-NC-6                Software shall disconnect from the Internet on startup.
 SW-SSD-1               The software shall support the following system state definitions:
 SW-SSD-1.2             Startup – state during which software initializes and attempts to connect to devices and stop any services related to remote access to
                        the Solea system. During this state the system checks devices and reports:
 SW-NC-1.3              The software shall allow the user to delete Wi-Fi credentials stored in the system.

SYSTEM CONFIGURATION REQUIREMENTS: SYSTEM CONFIGURATION BACKUP
REQUIREMENTS
       Req ID                                             System Configuration Requirements: System Configuration Backup
 SW-BA-1                The software shall backup the following items:
                        - The Solea Database
                        - The Solea Application Configuration Files
                        - The Solea Subscription Information File
                        - The Solea CAN module firmware configurations and calibration settings
                        - The PCON module firmware configuration settings.
 SW-BA-2                The software shall initiate the backup process without the user being notified after 7 days or more has expired since last backup and
                        the software has been idle in Standby for 15 minutes or longer.
 SW-BA-2.1              The backup files shall be stored on the Solea.
 SW-BA-2.2              The software shall upload the backup files to the Convergent Backup server.

                        (Note: Backup does not immediately trigger an upload. Upload occurs during the next log upload)
 SW-BA-2.3              The software shall remove local backup files that are older than 12 months at software startup.
 SW-BA-2.4              Ongoing backup shall be cancelled and network communication shall be disabled when the software exits Standby state.
 SW-BA-3                The software shall allow the service technician to initiate the backup process.
 SW-BA-3.1              The service technician shall be allowed to name the backup file.

SYSTEM CONFIGURATION REQUIREMENTS: SYSTEM CONFIGURATION RESTORE
REQUIREMENTS
       Req ID                                             System Configuration Requirements: System Configuration Restore
 SW-RE-1                The software shall allow the service technician to restore settings from backed up files.
 SW-RE-2                The software shall only allow restoring settings from a backup file that was created with the same version as the running software.
 SW-RE-3                The software shall download backup files from the Convergent Backup server if they don't exist locally on the Solea.
 SW-RE-4                The software shall display a list of backups ordered by date and allow user to select one backup to restore settings from.
 SW-RE-5                The software shall display and allow the following items to be restored:
                        - Galvo coordinates
                        - Users and favorites
                        - Subscription information
                        - Deviations
                        - Firmware calibration and configuration settings for each CAN module
                        - Firmware calibration and configuration settings for the PCON module
                        - Pattern settings
 SW-RE-5.1              The software shall allow a selection of one or more setting options to restore.
 SW-RE-5.2              Deviation shall be selected by default to be restored
 SW-RE-5.3              Pattern settings shall be selected by default to be restored
 SW-RE-6                The software shall create a backup prior to restoring settings.
 SW-RE-7                The software shall restore galvo coordinates from the backup to the Solea database if selected.
 SW-RE-8                The software shall restore all users and associated favorites if selected.
 SW-RE-9                The software shall restore the subscription settings and subscription information file if selected.
 SW-RE-10               The software shall download deviation definitions from the Convergent Backup server.
 SW-RE-10.1             A deviation definition contains one or more of the following to be restored:
                        - Solea Database Parameter
                        - Solea Application Configuration Parameter
                        - Firmware Configuration Parameter
                                                                                                                                                Page 107 of 285
Verification Test Protocol Template, 731-00006 Rev. D

                                                        COMPANY PROPRIETARY AND CONFIDENTIAL
                                                                                                             Solea Model 3 Software Design Verification Protocol
                                                                                                                                              790-00134 Rev. V

 SW-RE-10.2               If a deviation is selected to be restored the software shall extract the values for the parameters in the deviation definition from the
                          selected backup files and restore it.
 SW-RE-11                 The software shall restore all the CAN firmware configuration and calibration for a CAN module if selected.
 SW-RE-12                 The software shall restore all pattern settings if selected.

AUDITING / TROUBLESHOOTING: APPLICATION LOG
REQUIREMENTS
       Req ID                                                         Auditing / Troubleshooting: Application Log
 SW-AL-1                The software shall record an entry in the application log whenever any of the following actions begin:
 SW-AL-1.1              Log on.
 SW-AL-1.2              Perform laser cutting
 SW-AL-1.4              Modify the system sound volume.
 SW-AL-2                The information recorded in the application log shall include the following information:
 SW-AL-2.1              Date/time the action occurred.
 SW-AL-2.2              Name of the action.
 SW-AL-2.3              Values for parameters or settings, if appropriate, for the action type.
 SW-AL-3                The software shall record any error events in the application log file with the following information:
 SW-AL-3.1              Date/Time the error occurred.
 SW-AL-3.3              Error description.
 SW-AL-3.4              Error code
 SW-AL-4                The software shall support the ability to export the log database in delimited-text format, and copy resulting application logs to a USB
                        device by exiting the Software and performing the copy using standard utilities provided by the Windows operating system.

AUDITING / TROUBLESHOOTING: ERROR LOG
REQUIREMENTS
       Req ID                                                            Auditing / Troubleshooting: Error Log
 SW-EL-1                The software shall record an entry in a separate error log row whenever an error occurs. The following information should be
                        recorded with each error:
 SW-EL-1.1              Date and time of occurrence.
 SW-EL-1.2              Error code
 SW-EL-1.4              The software state at the time of error occurrence.
 SW-CL-1                When in the Laser Cutting state, the software shall log the following information every second into a separate log file:
 SW-CL-1.1              Handpiece selection
 SW-CL-1.2              Pattern selection (note, includes type and spot size for example low power 1.25mm)
 SW-CL-1.3              Mist value selection
 SW-CL-1.4              Air state (on or off) selection
 SW-CL-1.5              Max cutting speed selection
 SW-CL-1.6              Cutting speed
 SW-CL-1.7              Air sheath pressure selection

REQUIREMENTS
      Req ID                                                     Auditing / Troubleshooting: View Upload Usage Information
 SW-WVU-1               The software shall provide the ability for the Service Technician role to view laser usage information for the system. The information
                        displayed shall include the following:
 SW-WVU-1.1             The total time the laser has been activated as measured by the time spent in the states Laser User Warning, Laser Armed, and Laser
                        Cutting states.
 SW-WVU-1.2             The average time of laser activation, as measured by dividing the total activation time by the number of activations.
 SW-WVU-1.3             The activation information shall be stored in the Windows registry such that it is not removed/replaced during re-installation of the
                        Solea software.
 SW-WVU-2               The software shall provide the ability to capture the following hand piece usage information and upload this usage information to the
                        Convergent Installation Server.
 SW-WVU-2.1             The number of times a specific hand piece type is used on the system
 SW-WVU-2.2             The amount of time a specific hand piece type is used

AUDITING / TROUBLESHOOTING: UPLOAD OF LOGS TO CLOUD
REQUIREMENTS
       Req ID                                                    Auditing / Troubleshooting: Upload of Logs to Cloud
 SW-UL-1                The software shall upload logs to the Convergent Log Server.
 SW-UL-2                The software shall compress the log files to zip file (s) using the device serial number and date/time stamp as file name prior to
                        uploading the compressed file(s) to the Convergent Log Server.
 SW-UL-3                The software shall only upload log files to the Convergent Log Server that have not been uploaded in the past.
 SW-UL-4                The software shall allow the User to initiate the log upload process.
 SW-UL-4.3              The software shall display the result of the upload process.
 SW-UL-4.1              The software shall display the progress of the upload
 SW-UL-4.2              The software shall allow the upload process to be cancelled.
 SW-UL-7                The software shall initiate the log upload process without the user being notified after one of following conditions:
 SW-UL-7.1              After a non-recoverable error occur
 SW-UL-7.2              7 days or more has expired since last log upload and the software has been idle in Standby for 15 minutes or longer.
 SW-UL-8                Ongoing log upload shall be cancelled and network communication shall be disabled when the software exits Standby state.

SW-UL-1, SW-UL-2, SW-UL-3, SW-UL-4, SW-UL-4.3, SW-UL-4.1, SW-UL-4.2, SW-UL-7.1, SW-UL-7.2, SW-UL-

AUDITING / TROUBLESHOOTING: ERROR CLASSIFICATION & ERROR HANDLING
REQUIREMENTS
       Req ID                                               Auditing / Troubleshooting: Error Classification & Error Handling
 SW-EC-1                The software shall classify the errors or issues into the following categories:
 SW-EC-1.1              Non-recoverable (example: faulty CAN adapter)
 SW-EH-1                When the software detects a non-recoverable error, it shall:
 SW-EH-1.2              Present a notification to the user that include:
 SW-EH-1.2.1            Error code and error description
 SW-EH-1.2.2            Instructions that complete shutdown is required (main power in the back must be switched off)
 SW-EH-1.2.3            Client Services contact information
 SW-EH-1.3              Add error to the Error log
 SW-EC-1.2              Recoverable (example: air pressure error)
 SW-EH-2                When the software detects a recoverable error in Standby state it shall attempt to recover automatically without notifying the user.
 SW-EH-2.3              Add error to the Error log
 SW-EC-1.3              Warning (example: water reservoir empty)
 SW-EH-3                When the software detects a warning level issue, it shall:
 SW-EH-3.1              Return the system to Standby state
 SW-EH-3.2              Present a notification to the user that include:
 SW-EH-3.2.1            Description of warning
 SW-EH-3.2.2            Specific Instructions of actions to be taken if applicable.
 SW-EH-4                If the recoverable error is detected in a state other than Standby state or if the automatic recovery failed, the software shall:
 SW-EH-4.1              Present a notification to the user that include:
 SW-EH-4.1.1            Error code and error description


                                                                                                                                                   Page 135 of 285
Verification Test Protocol Template, 731-00006 Rev. D

                                                         COMPANY PROPRIETARY AND CONFIDENTIAL
                                                                                                      Solea Model 3 Software Design Verification Protocol
                                                                                                                                       790-00134 Rev. V

 SW-EH-4.1.2             Specific Instructions of actions to be taken if applicable.
 SW-EH-4.1.3             Client Services contact information
 SW-EH-4.1.4             The option to attempt a new recovery attempt.
 SW-EH-4.2               Add error to the Error log

AUDITING / TROUBLESHOOTING: REMOTE ACCESS SPECIFIC BEHAVIOR
REQUIREMENTS
       Req ID                                                    Auditing / Troubleshooting: Remote Access Specific Behavior
 SW-RAS-1               The software shall allow the user to start the TeamViewer remote access service.
 SW-RAS-2               If the TeamViewer remote access service fails to start, the system will transition to the error state.
 SW-RAS-6               After successful startup of TeamViewer and establishing an Internet connection, the touch screen shall be disabled.
 SW-RAS-7               After successful startup of TeamViewer and establishing an Internet connection, the software shall not allow the laser to be
                        activated.

SW-RAS-1, SW-RAS-2, SW-RAS-6, SW-RAS-7
TESTING METHODS

 SW-HW-8                 The software shall display the status of the water reservoir level on the Home screen.
 SW-HW-9                 The water reservoir level status shall be displayed in one of four states – full, low, empty, or water reservoir removed.

 SW-HW-11                The software shall indicate when the water level drops to empty and the laser will be disabled and any cutting in progress stops.

STATE REQUIREMENTS: SYSTEM STATE DEFINITIONS
REQUIREMENTS
       Req ID                                                         State Requirements: System State Definitions
 SW-SSD-1               The software shall support the following system state definitions:
 SW-SSD-1.2             Startup – state during which software initializes and attempts to connect to devices and stop any services related to remote access to
                        the Solea system. During this state the system checks devices and reports:
 SW-SSD-1.2.3           Foot pedal not connected, reported as recoverable error.
 SW-SSD-1.2.7           Any failure to stop the TeamViewer system service, reported as non-recoverable error.
 SW-SSD-1.3             Standby – state after startup but before user has indicated they want to use the laser. This state is also returned to after laser
                        operation is complete or after successful error recovery..
 SW-SSD-1.4             Arming – state after Standby and after user has selected the Turn Laser On button. State where the system is primed (if applicable)
                        and presents a visible and audible warning to the user that the laser is about to be armed (activated).
 SW-SSD-1.6             Laser Armed – state in which the laser is ready but not cutting (pulsing). The Laser Armed indicator blinks while the system is in the
                        Laser Armed state. (Note: the User can deactivate the laser by selecting the Turn Laser Off button while in the Laser Armed state and
                        the system will return to the Standby state.)
 SW-SSD-1.7             Laser Cutting – state in which the laser is actively cutting (pulsing). The Laser Cutting indicator blinks while the system is in the Laser
                        Cutting state.
 SW-SSD-1.9             System Error – state when a non-recoverable error has been detected and no further laser operation is allowed for safety purposes.
 SW-SSD-1.10            Error Recovery – state when the system is attempting to recover from recoverable error. If successful, the system will return to
                        Standby state and allow continued operation.
 SW-AS-4                The software shall transition into the alignment phase when the foot pedal is pressed down
 SW-SSD-1.11            Alignment - the state during which the laser is firing while connected to the Perioguide Acquisition Module and the readings from the
                        Perioguide Sensor Module are interpreted to determine when the laser is centered within the Perioguide Tip.
 SW-SST-3.5             Alignment state, when the user selects to start an alignment procedure.

SW-SSD-1.2.3, SW-SSD-1.2.7, SW-SSD-1.3, SW-SSD-1.4, SW-SSD-1.6, SW-SSD-1.7, SW-SSD-1.9, SW-SSD-
      1.10, SW-SSD-1.11, SW-SST-3.5, SW-AS-4

STATE REQUIREMENTS: SYSTEM STATE TRANSITIONS
REQUIREMENTS
       Req ID                                                        State Requirements: System State Transitions
 SW-SST-1               The software enters Startup state after reset/restart of system.
 SW-SST-2               The software shall support the following state transitions for exiting the Startup state:
 SW-SST-2.1             Standby, if all devices were started up successfully.
 SW-SST-2.2             Error, if startup fails.
 SW-SST-3               The software shall support the following state transitions for exiting the Standby state:
 SW-SST-3.1             Arming state when the Turn Laser On button is selected.
 SW-SST-3.2             Error, if a non-recoverable error is detected.
 SW-SST-3.4             Error Recovery, if a recoverable error is detected.
 SW-SST-5               The software shall support the following state transitions for exiting the Laser Armed state:
 SW-SST-5.1             Laser Cutting, when the foot pedal position is nonzero.
 SW-SST-5.1.1           If the foot pedal position was nonzero when entering Laser Armed state the software shall not allow transition to Laser Cutting state
                        until the foot pedal position has returned to zero position followed by a nonzero foot pedal position.
 SW-SST-5.2             Error, if a non-recoverable error is detected.
 SW-SST-5.4             Arming, if software detects that the user has:
 SW-SST-5.4.2           Changed the Mist value for the selected procedure from a value of zero to a value greater than zero.
 SW-SST-5.5             Standby when the Turn Laser Off button is selected.
 SW-SST-5.6             Error Recovery, if a recoverable error is detected.
 SW-SST-6               The software shall support the following state transitions for exiting the Laser Cutting state:
 SW-SST-6.1             Laser Armed, when the foot pedal position is 0.
 SW-SST-6.2             Error, if a non-recoverable error is detected.
 SW-SST-6.4             Error Recovery, if a recoverable error is detected.

                                                                                                                                               Page 154 of 285
Verification Test Protocol Template, 731-00006 Rev. D

                                                        COMPANY PROPRIETARY AND CONFIDENTIAL
                                                                                                           Solea Model 3 Software Design Verification Protocol
                                                                                                                                            790-00134 Rev. V

 SW-SST-7               The software shall support the following state transitions for exiting the Arming state:
 SW-SST-7.2             Laser Armed, when the arming operation is complete.
 SW-SST-7.4             Error, if a non-recoverable error is detected.
 SW-SST-7.5             Error Recovery, if a recoverable error is detected.
 SW-SST-7.6             Standby, if the user cancelled the arming process.
 SW-SST-8               The software shall support the following state transitions for exiting the Error Recovery state:
 SW-SST-8.1             Standby, if error recovery is successful
 SW-SST-8.2             Error, if error recovery failed
 SW-SST-12              When entering a new state, the software shall inform the following system modules to enter the new state.
 SW-SST-12.1            Mist Module
 SW-SST-12.2            Airflow Module
 SW-SST-12.3            Coolant Module
 SW-SST-12.4            Laser Power Module
 SW-SST-12.5            DC Power Monitor Module
 SW-SST-12.6            PCON, if applicable
 SW-SST-13              The software shall confirm that each system module specified in SW-SST-12 has entered the correct (new) state and, if not
                        confirmed, raise a non-recoverable error.
 SW-SST-14              The software shall support the following state transitions for exiting the Alignment state:
 SW-SST-14.1            Standby, when alignment is completed.
 SW-SST-14.2            Standby, if the user selects to cancel the alignment procedure.
 SW-SST-14.3            Error, if a non-recoverable error is detected.
 SW-SST-14.4            Error Recovery, if a recoverable error is detected.

                                                                                                                                          790-00134 Rev. V

TESTING METHODS

STATE REQUIREMENTS: SYSTEM STARTUP STATE SPECIFIC BEHAVIOR
REQUIREMENTS
       Req ID                                                 State Requirements: System Startup State Specific Behavior
 SW-SIB-1               When in the Startup state, the software shall initialize the following devices:
 SW-SIB-1.6             Foot Pedal
 SW-SIB-1.7             Laser Controller
                        Applies to LCD and PCON
 SW-SIB-1.8             Enable the touch screen
 SW-SIB-2               When in the Startup state, the software shall begin monitoring and continue monitoring until the software is shut down the following
                        system hardware devices:
 SW-SIB-2.1             CAN Communication with the following modules:
 SW-SIB-2.1.1           Mist Module
 SW-SIB-2.1.2           Airflow Module
 SW-SIB-2.1.3           Coolant Module
 SW-SIB-2.1.4           Laser Power Module
 SW-SIB-2.1.5           DC Power Monitor Module
 SW-SIB-2.2             USB communication with the PCON module
 SW-SIB-3               When in the Startup state and if PCON is used, the software shall verify that PCONs internal configuration matches the configuration
                        expected by the software.
                        Applies to PCON only.
 SW-SIB-3.1             The software shall issue a non-recoverable error if the configuration does not match.
 SW-SIB-4               When in the Startup state, the software shall at startup verify that patterns required by the software are installed on the LCD Laser
                        Controller.
                        Applies to LCD Only
 SW-SIB-4.1             The software shall install the pattern onto the Laser Controller in the case when the pattern is missing.


                                                                                                                                                Page 168 of 285
Verification Test Protocol Template, 731-00006 Rev. D

                                                        COMPANY PROPRIETARY AND CONFIDENTIAL
                                                                                                              Solea Model 3 Software Design Verification Protocol
                                                                                                                                               790-00134 Rev. V

 SW-SIB-4.2               If a pattern is missing after the software has attempted to install it to the Laser Controller, warn user and transition to the Error state.

STATE REQUIREMENTS: STANDBY STATE AND ERROR STATE SPECIFIC BEHAVIOR
REQUIREMENTS
       Req ID                                              State Requirements: Standby State and Error State Specific Behavior
 SW-SSB-1                When in the Standby state, the software shall prevent the user from firing the laser.
 SW-ES-1                 When in Error state the software shall turn the laser simmer off.
                         Applies to LCD Only
 SW-SSB-2                The laser simmer shall be turned on upon entry to the Standby state.
                         Applies to LCD Only

SW-SSB-1, SW-SSB-2, SW-ES-1

STATE REQUIREMENTS: ARMING STATE SPECIFIC BEHAVIOR
REQUIREMENTS
      Req ID                                                     State Requirements: Arming State Specific Behavior
 SW-LWB-1               When in the Arming state, the software shall prevent the user from firing the laser.
 SW-LWB-4               (LCD only) When in the Arming state, the software shall send the galvos to the laser "home" position using the saved x, y positions
                        from the most recent non-Perioguide galvo alignment operation when using a non-Perioguide handpiece selection.
 SW-LWB-5               The software shall stay in the Arming state for a minimum of 5 seconds.
 SW-LWB-5.1             The software shall prime the system for a minimum of 40 seconds if the time since last priming exceeds 12 hours if the mist water is
                        selected to be on.
 SW-LWB-5.2             The software shall prime the system for a minimum of 5 seconds if mist water is selected to be on.
 SW-LWB-5.3             The software shall allow the priming to be cancelled.
 SW-LWB-6               An audible and visible warning lasting a minimum of 5 seconds shall be presented to the user indicating that the laser is about to be
                        armed.
 SW-LWB-6.1             After completion of the Arming period, the audible and visible warnings shall stop and the software enters the Laser Armed state.
 SW-LWB-7               The software shall warn the user to put on their safety glasses in the Arming state.
 SW-LWB-8               (PCON only) When in the Arming state, the software shall send the saved x, y positions from the most recent non-Perioguide galvo
                        alignment operation as the home position to PCON when using a non-Perioguide handpiece selection.

                        Note: PCON will home the galvos when it is entering its Laser Armed state.
 SW-LWB-9               (Perioguide Only) During use of the Perioguide handpiece the software shall use the most recent galvo x,y positions that are saved for
                        the Perioguide handpiece.

SW-LWB-1, SW-LWB-4, SW-LWB-5, SW-LWB-5.1, SW-LWB-5.2, SW-LWB-5.3, SW-LWB-6, SW-LWB-6.1,
      SW-LWB-7, SW-LWB-8, SW-LWB-9
TESTING METHODS

STATE REQUIREMENTS: LASER ARMED STATE SPECIFIC BEHAVIOR
REQUIREMENTS
       Req ID                                                        State Requirements: Laser Armed State Specific Behavior
 SW-LUB-2                 When in the Laser Armed state, if the selected pattern is low-power, and the Mist value is 0, the user shall have the option to disable
                          and enable the use of Air.
 SW-LUB-2.1               After disabling air, if the user changes the Mist value back to a value greater than 0, the software shall warn the user that the system
                          shall be primed again.
 SW-LUB-2.2               After disabling air, if the user selects a standard mode favorite, the software shall warn the user that the system shall be primed
                          again.
 SW-LUB-3                 When in the Laser Armed state with air enabled, if the user changes the Mist value from 0 to a value greater than 0, the software
                          shall require the user to prime the system.
 SW-LUB-3.1               The software shall warn the user that the system shall be primed again.

 SW-LCB-1                When exiting in the Laser Cutting state (user fully removes foot from the foot pedal), the software shall set the laser "home" position
                         to the saved x, y positions from the most recent galvo alignment operation. If no galvo alignment has been performed, the home
                         position shall be set to 0, 0.

                         Note: The LCD is explicitly commanded to move the galvos to the home position while the PCON is homing the galvos when
                         commanded to go back to the Armed state.
 SW-LCB-1.1              During use of the Perioguide handpiece the software shall use the galvo x,y positions that are saved for the Perioguide handpiece.

STATE REQUIREMENTS: ERROR RECOVERY SPECIFIC BEHAVIOR
REQUIREMENTS
       Req ID                                                    State Requirements: Error Recovery Specific Behavior
 SW-ER-1                 When in the Error Recovery state, the software shall prevent the user from firing the laser.
 SW-ER-2                 When the software detects an "external air pressure low" error the software shall give the user the option to force the software use
                         of the internal compressor.
 SW-ER-2.1               The software shall provide the user the means to re-enable the user of external air.

STATE REQUIREMENTS: ALIGNMENT STATE SPECIFIC BEHAVIOR
REQUIREMENTS
       Req ID                                                   State Requirements: Alignment State Specific Behavior
 SW-AS-1                During the setup phase, the software shall:
 SW-AS-1.1              Provide the user with instructions on how to properly connect the Perioguide Sensor Module to the hand piece.
 SW-AS-1.2              Warn the user that the laser will fire during the alignment and instruct that safety glasses must be worn.
 SW-AS-1.3              Establish communication connection with the Acquisition Module.
 SW-AS-1.3.1            If the software identifies several Acquisition Modules, it will prompt the user to choose which one to establish communication with.
 SW-AS-1.4              Display the network name of the Acquisition Module it is connected to.
 SW-AS-1.5              Update the Perioguide Control Module Firmware if the module firmware version differs from the version that is included in the
                        software.
 SW-AS-2                The software shall allow the alignment phase to commence if all of the following conditions are met:
 SW-AS-2.1              The software has established communication with the Perioguide Acquisition Module.
 SW-AS-2.2              The software confirms that the Perioguide Sensor Module is connected to the Perioguide Acquisition Module.
 SW-AS-2.3              The Perioguide Acquisition Module battery level is not low.

                        Note: See Firmware Requirement FW-AF-3.
 SW-AS-12               The shutter shall be closed upon exiting the alignment phase.
 SW-AS-3                The software shall prompt the user and require confirmation with a screen tap before allowing transition into the alignment phase.
 SW-AS-3.1              After confirmation from the user, the software shall open the shutter.
 SW-AS-4                The software shall transition into the alignment phase when the foot pedal is pressed down
 SW-AS-4.1               If the foot pedal is pressed or held when the pre-alignment user confirmation is displayed or confirmed by the user, the software
                        shall not allow alignment to start until the foot pedal is released and pressed again.
 SW-AS-5                The software shall exit the alignment phase and be considered unsuccessful if any of the following conditions occur:


                                                                                                                                               Page 198 of 285
Verification Test Protocol Template, 731-00006 Rev. D

                                                        COMPANY PROPRIETARY AND CONFIDENTIAL
                                                                                                          Solea Model 3 Software Design Verification Protocol
                                                                                                                                           790-00134 Rev. V

 SW-AS-5.1              The foot pedal is released.
 SW-AS-5.2              The communication to the Perioguide Acquisition Module is lost.
 SW-AS-5.3              The Perioguide Sensor Module is disconnected from the Perioguide Acquisition Module.
 SW-AS-7                The software shall play a sound during the alignment.

                        Note: Sound played during alignment will be equivalent to sound played during laser cutting state (SW-SO-1).
 SW-AS-13               Once the alignment phase is completed the software transitions to the result phase.
 SW-AS-13.1             During the transition the software shall close the communication to the Perioguide Acquisition Module.
 SW-AS-14               The software shall display the result of the alignment to the user.
 SW-AS-6                The software shall display the progress of the alignment.
 SW-AS-16               Alignment shall be considered unsuccessful if no power is detected during alignment.
 SW-AS-10               (Scanning Phase) Upon completion of successful alignment the software shall store a galvo x,y position that was in the upper 25% of
                        detected sensor output to the database specifically to be used with the Perioguide handpiece.

                        Note: Scanning phase is contingent on Home Position Check being unsuccessful.
 SW-AS-8                The alignment phase shall not exceed 60 seconds.
 SW-AS-9                Air and mist shall be disabled during the alignment phase.
 SW-AS-11               The software shall provide the user with an option to retry the alignment if the alignment was unsuccessful.
 SW-AS-5.4              The user cancels the alignment on the GUI.
 SW-AS-15               Periodontal treatment shall be disabled if there are no stored galvo x, y positions for the Perioguide handpiece.
 SW-AS-17               (Home position check) Each alignment will begin at the home galvo positions and be considered successful if the sensor reading is
                        greater than 0.5 W. If sensor reading at home galvo position is less than or equal to 0.5 W, then the alignment will proceed to the
                        scanning phase. Upon completion of a successful home position check, the software shall store a Perioguide galvo x,y position equal
                        to the home galvo position.
 SW-AS-1.6              Verify Perioguide Control Module Firmware authenticity.

WORKFLOW REQUIREMENTS: INITIATION OF LASER CUTTING
REQUIREMENTS
      Req ID                                                      Workflow Requirements: Initiation of Laser Cutting
 SW-WLP-1               The Home screen shall be disabled if any of the following conditions exist:
 SW-WLP-1.1             The foot pedal is not connected.
 SW-WLP-1.2             The software is in Laser Cutting state.
 SW-WLP-1.3             The software is in Arming state.
 SW-WLP-1.4             The software is in Error state.
 SW-WLP-1.5             A handpiece is not selected
 SW-WLP-2               The software shall allow the user to set the following laser cutting parameters from the Home screen
 SW-WLP-2.1             Mist water flow rate
 SW-WLP-2.2             Spot size
 SW-WLP-25              The spot sizes that the user shall be able to select on the Home screen include:
                        - 0.25mm
                        - 0.50mm
                        - 0.75mm
                        - 1.00mm
                        - 1.25mm
                        - 1.50mm
                        - 7.00mm Hex (Sleep Only)
                        - 2.00mm Hex (Solea Protect Only)
 SW-WLP-2.3             Maximum cutting speed
 SW-WLP-2.4             Mist air (on/off)
 SW-WLP-2.5             Air sheath pressure
 SW-WLP-3               The software shall associate and display the following sets of favorites per handpiece type:

                         Favorite                   Handpiece     Power Mode       Spot Size (mm)    Mist (mL/min)     Air        Speed              Cycle Limit
                         Thick Enamel               Contra        Standard         1.25              9                 Medium     30%                N/A
                         Thin Enamel                Contra        Standard         1.25              9                 Medium     20%                N/A
                         Gingivectomy               Contra        Standard         1.25              8                 Medium     30%                N/A
                         Crown Lengthening          Contra        Standard         0.5               8                 Medium     30%                N/A
                         Fibroma Removal            Contra        Standard         1                 8                 Medium     30%                N/A
                         Implant Exposure           Contra        Standard         1.25              8                 Medium     30%                N/A
                         Operculectomy              Contra        Standard         1.25              8                 Medium     30%                N/A
                         Child/Adult Frenectomy     Contra        Standard         1                 8                 Medium     40%                N/A
                         Tooth Uncovery             Contra        Standard         1.25              8                 Medium     30%                N/A
                         Crown Removal              Contra        Standard         1.25              13                Medium     90%                N/A
                         Veneer Removal             Contra        Standard         1.25              13                Medium     80%                N/A
                         Biopsy                     Contra        Standard         1                 8                 Medium     30%                N/A
                         Aphthous Ulcer             Contra        Low Power        1.25              Off               Off        10%                N/A
                         Infant Frenectomy          Straight      Low Power        0.75              Off               Off        60%                N/A

                         Periodontal Therapy        Perioguide    Low Power        0.25              Low               On         50%                N/A


                        * 0 cycle limit means no limit on the number of cycles


 SW-WLP-4               The software shall allow a user to save and restore combinations of the laser cutting settings in the form or "favorites".
 SW-WLP-4.1             The user shall be allowed to delete a favorite
 SW-WLP-4.2             The user shall be allowed to copy a favorite
 SW-WLP-5               When selecting a favorite, the software shall set and display the following cutting parameters associated with the favorite:
 SW-WLP-5.1             Spot size
 SW-WLP-5.2             Mist water ratio
 SW-WLP-5.3             Air


                                                                                                                                                   Page 224 of 285
Verification Test Protocol Template, 731-00006 Rev. D

                                                        COMPANY PROPRIETARY AND CONFIDENTIAL
                                                                                                           Solea Model 3 Software Design Verification Protocol
                                                                                                                                            790-00134 Rev. V

 SW-WLP-5.3.1           Standard mode selections:
                        - Low (10 psi air sheath, 20 psi mist air)
                        - Medium (17 psi air sheath, 20 psi mist air)
                        - High (25 psi air sheath, 20 psi mist air)
 SW-WLP-5.3.2           Non- Perioguide Low Power model selections
                        - Off (0 psi air sheath, 0 psi mist air)
                        - Low (10 psi air sheath, 20 psi mist air)
                        - Medium (17 psi air sheath, 20 psi mist air)
                        - High (25 psi air sheath, 20 psi mist air )
 SW-WLP-5.3.3           Perioguide Low Power model selections
                        - Off (0 psi air sheath, 0 psi mist air)
                        - Low (7 psi air sheath, 8 psi mist air)
 SW-WLP-5.4             Cutting speed
 SW-WLP-5.5             Air sheath pressure
 SW-WLP-6               Contra-angle and Straight Handpiece
                        The mist flow rate shall be adjustable in 1.0 ml/min increments.

                        Perioguide Handpiece
                        The mist flow rate shall have a low, medium, and high setting.
                        Low – 3ml/min
                        Medium – 5 ml/min
                        High – 7 ml/min
 SW-WLP-6.1             (Contra-angle and Straight Handpiece only) The allowable flow rate range for low power mode favorites shall be 5-15 ml/min
 SW-WLP-6.2             (Contra-angle and Straight Handpiece only) The allowable flow rate range for standard mode favorites shall be 8-15 ml/min
 SW-WLP-6.3             The software shall allow the user to turn off mist water for low power mode favorites.
 SW-WLP-7               If the selected favorite is low power mode favorite, and the Mist value is 0, the user shall have the option with a single operation to
                        disable and enable the use of Air.
 SW-WLP-7.1             With air disabled in Laser Armed state, if the user changes the Mist value back to a value greater than 0, the user shall be warned
                        that Air is being re-enabled and the software shall re-enable Air.


                                                                                                                                                  Page 225 of 285
Verification Test Protocol Template, 731-00006 Rev. D

                                                        COMPANY PROPRIETARY AND CONFIDENTIAL
                                                                                                           Solea Model 3 Software Design Verification Protocol
                                                                                                                                            790-00134 Rev. V

 SW-WLP-7.2             With air disabled in Laser Armed state, if the user selects a standard mode favorite, the software shall warn the user that Air is being
                        re-enabled and the software shall re-enable Air.
 SW-WLP-10              The Turn Laser On button shall be disabled if any of the following conditions are true:
 SW-WLP-10.1            A valid handpiece is not selected to the system.
 SW-WLP-10.2            The laser is not ready.
                        Note: Applies to CX-15 lasers only
 SW-WLP-8               The software shall display on the Home screen the type of handpiece connected to the system, with the handpiece being on the
                        following supported types:
 SW-WLP-8.1             None (no recognized handpiece connected)
 SW-WLP-8.2             Contra Angle handpiece
 SW-WLP-8.3             Straight handpiece
 SW-WLP-8.5             Sleep handpiece
 SW-WLP-8.6             Solea Protect handpiece
 SW-WLP-8.7             Perioguide handpiece
 SW-WLP-10.3            A pattern has not been loaded to the laser controller
                        Note: Applies to both LCD and PCON
 SW-WLP-11              The software shall display a maximum of two pop-up dialogs screen upon laser activation.
 SW-WLP-15              Foot pedal input shall be translated into cutting parameter in accordance to the selected spot type.
 SW-WLP-15.1            The software shall interpret the foot pedal on a scale from 1-255 and map the foot pedal input value to the pulse duration.
 SW-WLP-16              The software shall have a delay of no more than 501 milliseconds from the time the user removes his/her foot from the foot pedal
                        until laser cutting is stopped.
 SW-WLP-17              The software shall have a delay of no more than 800 milliseconds when initiating laser firing when the user first presses the foot
                        pedal.
 SW-WLP-24              If mist is enabled, there shall be a delay of at least 400 miliseconds between when the user presses the footpedal and the software
                        initiates firing of the laser.
 SW-WLP-24.1            Prior to commanding the pulse width to the value specified by the foot pedal, the software shall increase the pulse width command
                        to 10% of the pulse width range over a period of 300 to 650 ms from the beginning of the first pulse.

                                                                                                                                                  Page 226 of 285
Verification Test Protocol Template, 731-00006 Rev. D

                                                        COMPANY PROPRIETARY AND CONFIDENTIAL
                                                                                                           Solea Model 3 Software Design Verification Protocol
                                                                                                                                            790-00134 Rev. V

 SW-WLP-24.1.1          The ramp shall not be executed if the ramp end-point pulse duration is less or equal to the user selected max pulse duration.
 SW-WLP-24.1.2          The ramp shall not be executed if the Sleep handpiece is selected.
 SW-WLP-24.1.3          The ramp shall not be executed if the Solea Protect handpiece is selected.
 SW-WLP-18              The user shall not be allowed to change any parameters, such as spot size or cutting speed, either through selection of favorites or
                        otherwise, nor will they be able to navigate to other areas of the application while the foot pedal is pressed and cutting is in progress.
 SW-WLP-19              If a user fully removes his/her foot from the foot pedal, all current selections such as spot size and maximum cutting speed will
                        remain in effect.
 SW-WLP-23              When the user selects Low Power, an informational message shall be displayed to the user describing the purpose and behavior of
                        low power mode.
 SW-WLP-23.1            The user shall have the option to prevent the informational message from being displayed again.

      Req ID                                                           Workflow Requirements: Palatal Coagulation
 SW-WLP-26              When the user selects the Sleep handpiece the software shall display a dialog that:
 SW-WLP-26.1            Instructs the user to verify that Sleep handpiece cartridge has been inserted.
 SW-WLP-26.2            Instructs the user to perform a burn card test.
 SW-WLP-26.3            [Removed in Rev. U]
 SW-WLP-26.4            [Removed in Rev. U]
 SW-WLP-26.5            [Removed in Rev. U]
 SW-WLP-27              If the Sleep handpiece is selected software shall not display or allow the mist and air to be adjusted.
 SW-WLP-28              If the Sleep handpiece is selected the software shall allow the user to limit number times a pattern is fired to:
                        - No Limitation
                        -2

                        Note: The software will stop the laser after the selected limit even if foot pedal is pressed. To fire again the user must take the foot of
                        the foot pedal and press it again.
 SW-WLP-29              If the Sleep handpiece is selected the software shall display a counter of how many times the pattern has been executed.
 SW-WLP-29.1            The user shall be able to reset the counter.
 SW-WLP-30              When executing the Palatal Coagulation pattern, the pulse width shall be fixed at the pulse width corresponding to the governor
                        position.

 SW-PF-1                  The user shall be able to login within 30 seconds after start of the application. Note that this is form the time the application is

 FW-PF-2                  The firmware shall control the air exhaust valve to maintain the pre-regulator pressure at a set point pressure
 SW-PF-2                  The software shall exit cutting state no later than 0.5s after the foot pedal is disconnected.

             button. Step on the foot pedal to enter Laser

             the pulse width of the laser modulation signal

WORKFLOW REQUIREMENTS: DEMONSTRATION MODE
REQUIREMENTS
      Req ID                                                        Workflow Requirements: Demonstration Mode
 SW-DM-1                The software shall display a notification when the system is in Demonstration Mode that the system shall not be used on patients.
 SW-DM-2                The software shall suppress fault codes defined in a configuration file when the system is in Demonstration Mode.
 SW-DM-3                The software shall communicate to the CAN modules the errors to ignore when Demo mode is enabled.
 SW-DM-4                The software shall communicate to the CAN modules that no errors should be ignored when Demo mode is disabled.
